## PTSD and DSM-5 by National Center for PTSD

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by National Center for PTSD](psychiatry.md#work-by-national-center-for-ptsd)

[https://www.ptsd.va.gov/professional/treat/essentials/dsm5_ptsd.asp](https://www.ptsd.va.gov/professional/treat/essentials/dsm5_ptsd.asp)

Current official diagnostic explainer, used only to corroborate the DSM-5 qualifying-[exposure](causal-inference.md#exposure) and full-syndrome requirements already available before the exam. The served page also discusses later revision metadata; it is not presented as a pre-2022 research publication.

## Prospective Investigation of Stress Inoculation in Young Monkeys by Karen J. Parker and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Karen J. Parker](neuroscientist.md#work-by-karen-j-parker)

[https://doi.org/10.1001/archpsyc.61.9.933](https://doi.org/10.1001/archpsyc.61.9.933)

[https://med.stanford.edu/content/dam/sm/parkerlab/documents/Parker_JAMA_2004.pdf](https://med.stanford.edu/content/dam/sm/parkerlab/documents/Parker_JAMA_2004.pdf)

Primary randomized squirrel-monkey experiment, 20 animals, intermittent [exposure](causal-inference.md#exposure) during postnatal weeks 17–27 and subsequent behavioral/endocrine tests. Human psychiatric prevention is not directly measured.

## Whatever Does Not Kill Us by Mark D. Seery and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Mark D. Seery](psychologist.md#work-by-mark-d-seery)

[https://doi.org/10.1037/a0021344](https://doi.org/10.1037/a0021344)

[https://escholarship.org/uc/item/4b6787gk](https://escholarship.org/uc/item/4b6787gk)

Original subtitle: Cumulative Lifetime [Adversity](psychology.md#adversity), Vulnerability, and [Psychological resilience](psychology.md#psychological-resilience). Primary multiyear human observational study; curved associations are not a randomized [adversity](psychology.md#adversity)-dose recommendation.

## Depression and sensitization to stressors among young women as a function of childhood adversity by Constance Hammen and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Constance Hammen](psychologist.md#work-by-constance-hammen)

[https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-006X.68.5.782](https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-006X.68.5.782)

[https://hammenlab.psych.ucla.edu/pubs/00depressionand.pdf](https://hammenlab.psych.ucla.edu/pubs/00depressionand.pdf)

Original author-hosted paper; two-year prospective [psychological stress](psychology.md#psychological-stress)-sensitization test. The precise biological mechanism was not established by the observational result.

## Psychiatric resilience: longitudinal twin study by Ananda B. Amstadter and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Ananda B. Amstadter](psychologist.md#work-by-ananda-b-amstadter)

[https://doi.org/10.1192/bjp.bp.113.130906](https://doi.org/10.1192/bjp.bp.113.130906)

[https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4180845/](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4180845/)

Primary two-wave [twin study](genetics.md#twin-study) with an [exposure](causal-inference.md#exposure)-conditioned symptom-[regression residual](probability-and-statistics.md#regression-residual) [phenotype](genetics.md#phenotype). Wave-specific and latent longitudinal [heritability](genetics.md#heritability) estimates concern different modeled [variance](variance.md) components.

## Competence in the context of adversity by Ann S. Masten and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Ann S. Masten](psychologist.md#work-by-ann-s-masten)

[https://doi.org/10.1017/S0954579499001996](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0954579499001996)

[https://moodle4.cs.huji.ac.il/hu14/pluginfile.php/179677/mod_resource/content/1/Masten_et_al_1999.pdf](https://moodle4.cs.huji.ac.il/hu14/pluginfile.php/179677/mod_resource/content/1/Masten_et_al_1999.pdf)

Original subtitle: Pathways to [psychological resilience](psychology.md#psychological-resilience) and maladaptation from childhood to late adolescence. Primary ten-year, multimethod follow-up of 205 children; intellectual and [parenting](psychology.md#parenting) resources were examined.

## Relationships between poverty and psychopathology by E. Jane Costello and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by E. Jane Costello](biology.md#work-by-e-jane-costello)

[https://doi.org/10.1001/jama.290.15.2023](https://doi.org/10.1001/jama.290.15.2023)

[https://scholars.duke.edu/publication/757769](https://scholars.duke.edu/publication/757769)

Original subtitle: a [natural experiment](causal-inference.md#natural-experiment). Primary longitudinal income-change analysis; effects differed between behavioral and [anxiety](psychology.md#anxiety)/[depression](psychology.md#depression) symptoms. Author institutional record supplies original abstract.

## Attachment security as a mechanism linking foster care placement to improved mental health outcomes in previously institutionalized children by Katie A. McLaughlin and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Katie A. McLaughlin](psychologist.md#work-by-katie-a-mclaughlin)

[https://doi.org/10.1111/j.1469-7610.2011.02437.x](https://doi.org/10.1111/j.1469-7610.2011.02437.x)

[https://acamh.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1111/j.1469-7610.2011.02437.x](https://acamh.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1111/j.1469-7610.2011.02437.x)

Primary randomized foster-care-offer analysis: 136 children, attachment at 42 months, internalizing outcomes at 54 months. Online 2011, journal issue 2012. The mediator itself was not randomized.

## School-Based Prevention of Depressive Symptoms by Jane E. Gillham and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Jane E. Gillham](psychologist.md#work-by-jane-e-gillham)

[https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-006X.75.1.9](https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-006X.75.1.9)

[https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17295559/](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17295559/)

Original subtitle: A randomized controlled study of the effectiveness and specificity of the [Penn Resiliency Program](biology.md#penn-resiliency-program). Original author-hosted abstract and PubMed record verify 697 children, three arms and heterogeneous school results.

## Risk, resilience, and recovery by Emmy E. Werner

🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Emmy E. Werner](psychologist.md#work-by-emmy-e-werner)

Original subtitle: Perspectives from the Kauai [Longitudinal study](causal-inference.md#longitudinal-study). Author account of the 1955 birth-cohort follow-up, Development and Psychopathology 5, 503–515. Hosting/upload dates are not the research publication date.

## Ordinary magic by Ann S. Masten

🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Ann S. Masten](psychologist.md#work-by-ann-s-masten)

[https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/11315249/](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/11315249/)

Original subtitle: [Psychological resilience](psychology.md#psychological-resilience) processes in development. Conceptual synthesis concerning ordinary adaptive systems, not a new randomized experiment.

## Development of a new resilience scale by Kathryn M. Connor and Jonathan R. T. Davidson

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Jonathan R. T. Davidson](psychiatry.md#work-by-jonathan-r-t-davidson), [Work by Kathryn M. Connor](psychiatry.md#work-by-kathryn-m-connor)

[https://doi.org/10.1002/da.10113](https://doi.org/10.1002/da.10113)

Original subtitle: The [Connor-Davidson Resilience Scale](psychology.md#connor-davidson-resilience-scale) ([CD-RISC](psychology.md#connor-davidson-resilience-scale)). Original 25-item validation and treatment-associated change; no scale items are reproduced.

## Resilience to Major Life Stressors Is Not as Common as Thought by Frank J. Infurna and Suniya S. Luthar

Original reanalysis of longitudinal trajectories; within-class [variance](variance.md) and slope restrictions affect classification and estimated class sizes.

## Resilience to loss and chronic grief by George A. Bonanno and others

🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by George A. Bonanno](psychologist.md#work-by-george-a-bonanno)

[https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-3514.83.5.1150](https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-3514.83.5.1150)

[https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12416919/](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12416919/)

Original subtitle: a prospective study from preloss to 18-months postloss. Primary preloss and follow-up observations of 205 bereaved participants; no original graph is reproduced.

## The Construct of Resilience by Suniya S. Luthar and others

🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Suniya S. Luthar](psychologist.md#work-by-suniya-s-luthar)

[https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1885202/](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1885202/)

Original title continues: A Critical Evaluation and Guidelines for Future Work. Conceptual critique and methodological recommendations, not a new intervention trial.

## Russian blues reveal effects of language on color discrimination by Jonathan Winawer and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Jonathan Winawer](neuroscientist.md#work-by-jonathan-winawer)

[https://bpb-us-e1.wpmucdn.com/wp.nyu.edu/dist/4/2136/files/2017/01/winawer_witthoft_et_al_PNAS_2007.pdf](https://bpb-us-e1.wpmucdn.com/wp.nyu.edu/dist/4/2136/files/2017/01/winawer_witthoft_et_al_PNAS_2007.pdf)

PNAS 104, 7780–7785. Simultaneous matching and verbal versus spatial interference. An online publication date in April 2007 precedes the May issue date; both are before the examination.

## Color categories are not universal: Replications and new evidence from a stone-age culture by Debi Roberson and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Debi Roberson](psychologist.md#work-by-debi-roberson)

[https://citeseerx.ist.psu.edu/document?doi=c429668ad0f07e087354842dd643b4247b1e67ad&repid=rep1&type=pdf](https://citeseerx.ist.psu.edu/document?doi=c429668ad0f07e087354842dd643b4247b1e67ad&repid=rep1&type=pdf)

Journal of Experimental Psychology: General 129, 369–398. Naming, recognition, response-bias controls and Berinmo/English category comparisons. The historical title is retained without adopting its terminology for people.

## Biological origins of color categorization by Alice E. Skelton and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Alice E. Skelton](psychologist.md#work-by-alice-e-skelton)

[https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5448184/](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5448184/)

PNAS 114, 5545–5550. Infant recognition-memory measures and color-category structure; prelinguistic evidence is not equated with proof of no experience effects.

## The perception of gloss depends on highlight congruence with surface shading by Juno Kim and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Juno Kim](psychologist.md#work-by-juno-kim)

[https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/21841140/](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/21841140/)

Journal of Vision 11(9):4, 1–19. Primary abstract and original-paper text on position and orientation congruence and controls.

## Image statistics and the perception of surface qualities by Isamu Motoyoshi and others

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Isamu Motoyoshi](psychologist.md#work-by-isamu-motoyoshi)

[https://www.nature.com/articles/nature05724](https://www.nature.com/articles/nature05724)

Nature 447, 206–209. Primary publisher information on surface-image manipulations, skewness, [lightness](optics.md#lightness) and [gloss](neuroscience.md#gloss). This is not treated as a proof that a histogram uniquely determines appearance.

## Real-world illumination and the perception of surface reflectance properties by Roland W. Fleming and others

🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Roland W. Fleming](psychologist.md#work-by-roland-w-fleming)

[https://cs.stanford.edu/people/rondror/papers/fleming_jov_03.pdf](https://cs.stanford.edu/people/rondror/papers/fleming_jov_03.pdf)

Journal of Vision 3, 347–368. [Reflectance](radiometry.md#reflectance) matching under natural and altered illumination statistics.

## Mechanisms of color constancy under nearly natural viewing by James M. Kraft and David H. Brainard

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by David H. Brainard](psychologist.md#work-by-david-h-brainard), [Work by James M. Kraft](psychologist.md#work-by-james-m-kraft)

[https://color2.psych.upenn.edu/brainard/papers/Kraft.pdf](https://color2.psych.upenn.edu/brainard/papers/Kraft.pdf)

PNAS 96, 307–312. Physical chamber, controlled illumination and surfaces, projection colorimeter and achromatic adjustments.

<h2 id="the-spectral-sensitivities-of-the-middle-and-long-wavelength-sensitive-cones-derived-from-measurements-in-observers-of-known-genotype-by-andrew-stockman-and-others">The spectral sensitivities of the middle- and long-wavelength-sensitive cones derived from measurements in observers of known genotype by Andrew Stockman and others</h2>

↑ **Parent:** [Psychology bibliography](psychology-bibliography.md)  
🏷️ **Tags:** [Work by Andrew Stockman](psychologist.md#work-by-andrew-stockman)

[https://doi.org/10.1016/S0042-6989(00)00021-3](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0042-6989(00)00021-3)

Vision Research 40, 1711–1737. Primary paper consulted through a full original-paper PDF: [https://citeseerx.ist.psu.edu/document?doi=67190ada932581fa6e12e7e01005d496df293a88&repid=rep1&type=pdf.](https://citeseerx.ist.psu.edu/document?doi=67190ada932581fa6e12e7e01005d496df293a88&repid=rep1&type=pdf.) Heterochromatic flicker measurements and genotype-informed estimates; no post-2022 formula update is used.
